Let's design a rotation word generator:
```
def make_sturmian(M, alpha, beta, target=M//2):
    z = (np.arange(M)*alpha + beta) % 1.0
    bits = (z < 0.5).astype(float)
    cnt = bits.sum()
    if cnt > target:
       # turn excess ones to zero at positions with largest local 1r? Maybe random
       idx = np.where(bits==1)[0]
       # sort idx by something? choose those near ones? random
       bits[idx[:cnt-target]] = 0
    elif cnt<target:
       idx = np.where(bits==0)[0]
       bits[idx[:target-cnt]]=1
    return bits
```

We'll implement `cross_values(h,n)` returns list/array `vals` length 2n-1 in order [pos0, pos1,neg1, pos2,neg2,...]. Compute:
```
vals = np.empty(2*n-1)
vals[0] = np.dot(h,1-h)
idx=1
for L in range(1,n):
    vals[idx] = np.dot(h[:n-L], 1-h[L:]); idx+=1
    vals[idx] = np.dot(h[L:], 1-h[:n-L]); idx+=1
```
For f_neg: ∑_{i=L}^{n-1} h_i (1-h_{i-L}) = dot(h[L:],1-h[:n-L]). Good.
Grad computation using weights same order. Instead of looping over weights for f_pos/neg separately maybe same index. We'll write:
```
def cross_grad(weights, h,n):
 grad=np.zeros(n)
 p0=weights[0]
 grad += p0*(1-2*h)
 idx=1
 for L in range(1,n):
   pp=weights[idx]; pn=weights[idx+1]
   if abs(pp)>0: 
     grad[:n-L] += pp*(1-h[L:])
     grad[L:] += -pp*h[:n-L]
   if abs(pn)>0:
     grad[L:] += pn*(1-h[:n-L])
     grad[:n-L] += -pn*h[L:]
   idx +=2
 return grad
```
This uses slices; careful `grad[:n-L] += pp*(1-h[L:])` length n-L; `grad[L:]` length n-L. Good.

L-BFGS-B objective for a:
```
def sym_obgrad(a, alpha, penalty, n_half=100):
    h = build_sym_h(a)
    vals = cross_values(h,n)
    m=vals.max()
    s=alpha*(vals-m)
    smax=s.max()
    exps=np.exp(s-smax)
    p=exps/exps.sum()
    g_counts = dx * cross_grad(p,h,n)  # dx maybe 2/n
    grad_a = fold_g(g_counts)
    if penalty>0:
       diff = a.sum()-50.0
       g_counts? val += penalty*diff*diff
       grad_a += 2*penalty*diff
    val = dx*(m + (smax + np.log(exps.sum()))/alpha)
    # lse = smax + log(exps.sum()) with smax after exps? Actually if exps based on s-smax, lse = smax + log(exps.sum()).
    val += penalty*diff*diff
    return val, grad_a
```

```
def sym_fun(x, alpha, penalty, half):
    h = build_sym_h(x, half)
    vals = cross_vals(h, 4*half)
    m = vals.max()
    s = alpha*(vals-m)
    smax = s.max()
    e = np.exp(s-smax)
    p = e/e.sum()
    g = cross_grad(p,h,4*half)
    dx = 1.0/half  # since n=4half, dx=2/n = 1/(2half)? Wait n=400 when half100, dx=2/400=0.005 =1/200 =1/(2*half). Not 1/half. Let's compute: half=100, n=400, dx=0.005 = 1/(4half/2)=1/(2half)=1/200. Yes dx=1/(2*half).
    obj = dx*(m + (smax + np.log(e.sum()))/alpha)
    diff = x.sum() - half*0.5
    obj += penalty*diff*diff
    grad = dx*fold(g) + 2*penalty*diff
    return obj, grad
```

Projected gradient sym:

```
def pg_sym(a, half, alpha, deadline, lr_base, mu):
   H=2*half; n=4*half; dx=1/(2*half)
   x=a.copy(); best=x; bestc=eval
   mom=zeros
   lr=lr_base
   stall=0
   while time<deadline:
      vals=m? use alpha; compute p,g counts
      g_CA = dx*fold(count grad)
      mom = mu*mom - lr*g_CA
      x_new=project_sum(x+mom, half)
      c=eval(build_sym_h(x_new))
      if c<bestc: best=x_new; lr*=1.01
      else: stall; if stall>50 reduce lr etc.
   return best,bestc
```
Need top-level no closures.

```
      if c<best: accept, reset stall
      else accept with prob exp(-(c-best)/T) if annealing
   # final project? bits exact.
```

Let's detail candidate generation count adjustment for Markov bits:
```
def adjust_bits(bits, nbits, target, rng):
   b=bits.copy(); cnt=b.sum()
   if cnt>target:
      idx=np.where(b==1)[0]
      rng.shuffle(idx)
      for k in range(cnt-target): b[idx[k]]=0
   else:
      idx=np.where(b==0)[0]; shuffle; for...
   return b
```
This may create adjacent flips but okay.

Top-level helpers (no lambdas):
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_sum(x, target):
    n = len(x)
    x = np.clip(x,0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff = target - x.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff >0:
          room=1-x; s=room.sum()
          if s<1e-13: break
          x += diff/s*room
       else:
          s=x.sum()
          if s<1e-13: break
          x += diff/s*x
    return np.clip(x,0,1)
```
For full n target n/2; for a target half/2.

`project_feasible(h,n)` similar target n/2. Use project_sum.

`build_sym_h100(a)`? To generalize half:
```
def build_sym_h(a, half):
    s = np.concatenate((a, a[::-1]))
    return np.concatenate((s, s))
```

`cross_vals(h,n)`:
```
def cross_vals(h,n):
    vals=np.empty(2*n-1)
    vals[0]=np.dot(h,1-h)
    idx=1
    for L in range(1,n):
       vals[idx]=np.dot(h[:n-L], 1-h[L:]); idx+=1
       vals[idx]=np.dot(h[L:], 1-h[:n-L]); idx+=1
    return vals
```
Could use `one_minus_h = 1.0-h` before loop to save.
```
om = 1.0-h
vals[idx]=np.dot(h[:n-L], om[L:])
...
```

`cross_grad(weights,h,n)`:
```
def cross_grad(weights,h,n):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    p0=weights[0]
    if p0: g += p0*(1-2*h)
    idx=1
    for L in range(1,n):
       pp=weights[idx]; pn=weights[idx+1]
       m=n-L
       if pp:
          g[:m] += pp*(1-h[L:])
          g[L:] += -pp*h[:m]
       if pn:
          g[L:] += pn*(1-h[:m])
          g[:m] += -pn*h[L:]
       idx +=2
    return g
```
Check `1-h[L:]` length m; use `om = 1-h`; `om[L:]`, `om[:m]`.

`softmax_weights(vals, alpha)`: returns obj_counts and weights.
```
def softmax_counts(vals, alpha):
    m=vals.max()
    s=alpha*(vals-m)
    smax=s.max()
    e=np.exp(s-smax)
    total=e.sum()
    p=e/total
    obj=m+(smax+np.log(total))/alpha
    return obj,p
```
If total underflow? e at least one 1. Good.

`c5_counts(vals)`: `vals.max() * 2.0 / len?!` But cross_vals elements counts; C = max*2/n. In sym_fun, h length n. Return C = dx*obj. For final, use np.correlate:
```
def eval_c5(h):
    n=len(h); return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full'))*2.0/n)
```
Need ensure h sum target; but eval okay.

`sym_fun(a, alpha, penalty, half)`:
```
def sym_fun(a, alpha, penalty, half):
    x=np.clip(a,0,1) # L-BFGS should bounds, but clip maybe? If clip, gradient discontinuity; don't clip inside? L-BFGS respects bounds; but numerical slight out. Use x=a.
    h=build_sym_h(x,half)
    n=4*half; dx=1.0/(2*half)
    vals=cross_vals(h,n)
    obj_counts,p=softmax_counts(vals,alpha)
    diff=x.sum()-0.5*half
    obj=dx*obj_counts + penalty*diff*diff
    g_counts=cross_grad(p,h,n)
    gs=g_counts[:2*half]+g_counts[2*half:]
    ga=gs[:half]+gs[::-1][:half]
    grad=dx*ga + 2.0*penalty*diff
    return obj, grad
```
`np.clip(a,0,1)` inside may break gradient at bounds but okay; L-BFGS passes within. To be safe, `if np.any(a<0 or >1): x=np.clip else x=a`.

Potential performance: cross_vals and cross_grad each loop 400; fun could call both separately? In sym_fun, we call cross_vals then cross_grad, so two loops. Could combine to one function returning vals,p,grad? We can modify `obsgrad` that loops vals, then weights, then grad. It still two loops. Fine.

L-BFGS-B with fun returning tuple and `bounds` list. Need `bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*half`, max 100 okay.

```
          # But sum may off; project always.
          if c_raw? no.
```

`pg_sym`:
```
def pg_sym(a, half, alpha, deadline, lr=0.2, mu=0.85):
   x=project_sum(a.copy(),0.5*half); best=x; bestc=eval...
   mom=np.zeros(half)
   c=bestc; stall=0
   while time<deadline:
      h=build_sym_h(x,half); n=4*half; dx=1/(2*half)
      vals=cross_vals(h,n); _,p=softmax_counts(vals,alpha)
      g_counts=cross_grad(p,h,n)
      gs=...; ga=...
      mom=mu*mom - lr*ga
      x2=project_sum(x+mom,half*0.5)
      c2=eval_c5(build_sym_h(x2,half))
      if c2<bestc-1e-12:
         best=x2; bestc=c2; x=x2; stall=0; lr=min(lr*1.02,5)
      else:
         stall+=1
         if stall>100:
            lr*=0.9; if lr<1e-4: lr=0.1; mu=max(mu-0.01,0.5); stall=0
   return best,bestc
```

Binary local search:
```
def binary_search_a(bits, half, deadline, rng, iters_per_sec? ):
   target=half//2
   x=bits.copy().astype(float)
   h=build_sym_h(x,half); bestc=eval_c5(h); best=x
   T=0.02? counts equivalent? We'll use C diff. start T=0.005.
   stall=0
   for step ...:
     if time>deadline: break
     ones=np.where(x>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.where(x<0.5)[0]
     i=ones[rng.randint(len(ones))]; j=zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]
     # optional choose j closer to i? random.
     x2=x.copy(); x2[i]=0; x2[j]=1
     c=eval_c5(build_sym_h(x2,half))
     if c<bestc: best; x; stall=0
     elif c<currentc + T and rng.rand()<exp(-(c-currentc)/T): x=x2; currentc=c
     else: stall++
     reduce T over time.
   # final local greedy: repeat while improvement: random 200 moves, keep if <best.
```

```
def full_fun(h,alpha,penalty):
   n=len(h); dx=2/n
   vals=cross_vals(h,n)
   obj_counts,p=softmax_counts(vals,alpha)
   diff=h.sum()-n/2
   obj=dx*obj_counts + penalty*diff*diff
   g=dx*cross_grad(p,h,n) + 2*penalty*diff
   return obj,g
```

Full polish function:
```
def polish_full(h, deadline, alphas=(20,200,2000)):
   n=len(h); best=project_feasible(h.copy(),n); bestc=eval_c5(best); x=best.copy()
   bounds=[(0,1)]*n
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time>deadline-20: break
      try: res=minimize(full_fun, x, args=(alpha,100.0), bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':150,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10})
      except: continue
      x2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c=eval_c5(x2)
      if c<bestc: best=x2; bestc=c; x=x2.copy()
   return best,bestc
```
`full_fun` args: penalty only, n from x. But L-BFGS-B may call with `x, alpha, penalty`. Good.

Need be careful with global variables? None.

Potential problem: `minimize` with `fun` returning tuple and `args` but `jac` not specified. In SciPy, for L-BFGS-B, if `fun` returns both f and g, `jac` should be omitted or `True`? Let's recall: `minimize(method='L-BFGS-B')`: parameters `fun`, `jac`. If `fun` returns a tuple `(f, g)`, `jac` is ignored? Documentation: For some methods, fun can return (f,g). In `minimize`, if `jac` is not provided, it checks if fun returns tuple: "For L-BFGS-B, if value_and_grad is True?" Actually `fun` in `minimize` can return `(f,g)` for L-BFGS-B, `SLSQP`? The `options` has? Let's verify: SciPy `minimize` doc: Parameters: fun callable, `x`, args. jac callable. If jac not specified and fun returns tuple, "In some methods, fun may be required to return both the objective and gradient". I think yes. To be safe, we can implement `value_grad=True`? L-BFGS-B options? Not. Alternatively separate `fun` and `jac` but avoid double compute by caching? Could use global cache; but no closures. Simpler: use SLSQP with finite diff? But L-BFGS-B tuple likely works. I have used `minimize(fun, x0, jac=False, method='L-BFGS-B')` where `fun` returns (f,g)`? Usually if you want L-BFGS-B with analytical grad, pass `jac=grad` separately. If fun returns tuple and jac not given, it treats as f? Let's check: In scipy `_minimize_l_bfgsb`, `res = minimize(method='L-BFGS-B', fun=fun,...)`; it may call `fun(x)` and if result tuple, sets `f, g = fun(x)`. I believe yes: `res = _minimize_lbfgsb(fun, x0, args=args, jac=jac, ...)`, in `_check_optimize_result`, before call, if jac is True? There is `scalar_or_fgid = pfunc(x, *args)`, then if `isinstance(scalar_or_fgid, tuple)` then f,g. So okay.

If `minimize` fails, `res.x` maybe. Use `res.x` in try.

Now candidate generation details. Let's write compact.

`markov_bits(nbits,p,rng,target)`:
```
def markov_bits(nbits, p, rng, target):
    b = np.empty(nbits)
    b[0] = rng.rand() < 0.5
    for i in range(1,nbits):
       if rng.rand() < p: b[i] = 1.0 - b[i-1]
       else: b[i] = b[i-1]
    cnt = int(b.sum())
    if cnt > target:
       idx = np.nonzero(b == 1.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          take = idx[:cnt-target]
          b[take] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
       idx = np.nonzero(b == 0.0)[0]
       if idx.size:
          take = idx[:target-cnt]
          b[take] = 1.0
    return b
```
Adjustment taking first indices might bias start. Better shuffle idx: `idx = rng.choice(idx, cnt-target, replace=False)`. Use. For large adjustment maybe okay.
The count after alternating may be far from target for extreme p; random adjustment can break. But p list moderate.

`random_bits(nbits,target,rng)`:
```
b=np.zeros(nbits); idx=rng.choice(nbits,target,replace=False); b[idx]=1
```

`rotation_bits(nbits,target,rng, alpha, beta)` similar: `z=(np.arange(nbits)*alpha+beta)%1; b=(z<0.5).astype(float); adjust`.

`continuous_cands`: sinusoids for k:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,100,endpoint=False)
a=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x + rng.rand()*2π)
a=project_sum(a,50)
```

Binary search: Need update best_h. Use `dx=1/(2*half)`.
```
def binary_search(a0, half, deadline, rng):
    a=a0.astype(float).copy(); h=build_sym_h(a,half)
    c=eval_c5(h); bestc=c; best_a=a.copy(); best_h=h.copy()
    curc=c; T=0.01; stall=0
    dx=1.0/(2.0*half)
    while time.time()<deadline:
       ones=np.nonzero(a>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(a<0.5)[0]
       if ones.size<1 or zeros.size<1: break
       i=int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)]); j=int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
       if i==j: continue
       oldi=float(a[i]); oldj=float(a[j]); newi=1.0-oldi; newj=1.0-oldj
       set_sym_bit(h,i,newi,half); set_sym_bit(h,j,newj,half)
       c2=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)
       accept = c2 < curc
       if not accept:
          diff=c2-curc
          if diff <= T and rng.rand() < np.exp(-diff/T): accept=True
       if accept:
          a[i]=newi; a[j]=newj
          if c2 < bestc:
             bestc=c2; best_a=a.copy(); best_h=h.copy()
          curc=c2; stall=0
       else:
          set_sym_bit(h,i,oldi,half); set_sym_bit(h,j,oldj,half)
          stall +=1
       if stall > 200:
          if rng.rand() < 0.5:
             a=best_a.copy(); h=best_h.copy(); curc=bestc
          else:
             # random perturb 2 swaps
             for _ in range(2):
                ones=np.nonzero(a>0.5)[0]; zeros=np.nonzero(a<0.5)[0]
                if ones.size and zeros.size:
                   i=...; j=...; old...; set; a=...
             curc=eval...
          stall=0; T=max(0.005, T*1.2)
       else:
          T=max(0.0005, T*0.9995)
    return best_h,bestc,best_a
```

Full code:
```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - x.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            x = x + diff / s * room
        else:
            s = x.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            x = x + diff / s * x
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
```
`project_feasible(h,n)` call `return project_sum(h, n*0.5)`.

`eval_c5`:
```
def eval_c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n == 0: return 1.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * 2.0 / n)
```

`build_sym_h`:
```
def build_sym_h(a, half):
    s = np.concatenate((a, a[::-1]))
    return np.concatenate((s, s))
```

`cross_vals` with n even. Ensure `om` float.
```
def cross_vals(h,n):
    vals=np.empty(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    om=1.0-h
    vals[0]=np.dot(h,om)
    idx=1
    for L in range(1,n):
       en=n-L
       vals[idx]=np.dot(h[:en], om[L:]); idx+=1
       vals[idx]=np.dot(h[L:], om[:en]); idx+=1
    return vals
```
For f_neg: h[L:] length en, om[:en] yes.

`cross_grad`:
```
def cross_grad(p,h,n):
 om=1.0-h
 g=np.zeros(n)
 p0=p[0]
 if p0 != 0.0: g += p0*(1.0-2.0*h)
 idx=1
 for L in range(1,n):
   en=n-L
   pp=p[idx]; pn=p[idx+1]; idx+=2
   if pp != 0.0:
      g[:en] += pp*om[L:]
      g[L:] += -pp*h[:en]
   if pn != 0.0:
      g[L:] += pn*om[:en]
      g[:en] += -pn*h[L:]
 return g
```
Check positive derivative for f_pos: ∂h_i for i<en: (1-h_{i+L})=om[L+?], slice om[L:en]. yes length en. For i>=L: -h_{i-L}, slice h[:en]. Good. Negative: f_neg[L]=∑_{i=0}^{en-1} h_{L+i}(1-h_i). Derivative wrt h_{L+i}: om_i => g[L:en] += pn*om[:en]; wrt h_i: -h_{L+i} => g[:en] += -pn*h[L:]. Good.

`softmax_counts`:
```
def softmax_counts(vals, alpha):
    m=float(vals.max())
    s=alpha*(vals-m)
    sm=float(s.max())
    e=np.exp(s-sm)
    tot=e.sum()
    if tot <= 0 or not np.isfinite(tot):
       p=np.zeros_like(vals); active=np.nonzero(vals==m)[0]; p[active]=1.0/len(active); return m,p
    p=e/tot
    obj=m+(sm+np.log(tot))/alpha
    return obj,p
```
If alpha tiny? alpha >0.

`sym_fun`:
```
def sym_fun(a, alpha, penalty, half):
    x=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a,dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h=build_sym_h(x, half)
    n=4*half; dx=1.0/(2.0*half)
    vals=cross_vals(h,n)
    obj_counts,p=softmax_counts(vals,alpha)
    diff=x.sum()-0.5*half
    obj=dx*obj_counts + penalty*diff*diff
    gc=cross_grad(p,h,n)
    gs=gc[:2*half]+gc[2*half:]
    ga=gs[:half]+gs[::-1][:half]
    grad=dx*ga + 2.0*penalty*diff
    return obj, grad
```
No clipping x; L-BFGS bounds. If finite but slightly out, build h out of [0,1]? L-BFGS should keep. To be safe, `x=np.clip(x,0,1)`? That alters gradient; but if line search tries outside, bounds prevent. Use no clip. But `nan_to_num` may map -1e-12 to0? nan_to_num only inf/nan. Good.

`polish_sym_seed`:
```
def polish_sym_seed(a, half, deadline):
    x=project_sum(a,0.5*half)
    best_h=build_sym_h(x,half); best_c=eval_c5(best_h); best_a=x.copy()
    alphas=(5.0,20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0,10000.0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(half)] # no lambda? use list comprehension? List comp okay not lambda? It's not a closure, but to be safe use `[ (0.0,1.0) ] * half` allowed.
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
       try:
          res=minimize(sym_fun, x, args=(alpha,100.0,half), bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':200, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-10})
          x2=res.x
       except Exception:
          continue
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x2)): continue
       x2=project_sum(x2,0.5*half)
       h2=build_sym_h(x2,half)
       c2=eval_c5(h2)
       if c2 < best_c:
          best_c=c2; best_a=x2.copy(); best_h=h2; x=x2.copy()
    return best_a,best_h,best_c
```

```
def full_fun(h, alpha, penalty):
    x=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(h,dtype=float), nan=0.5,...)
    n=len(x); dx=2.0/n
    vals=cross_vals(x,n)
    obj_counts,p=softmax_counts(vals,alpha)
    diff=x.sum()-0.5*n
    obj=dx*obj_counts + penalty*diff*diff
    gc=cross_grad(p,x,n)
    grad=dx*gc + 2.0*penalty*diff
    return obj, grad
```
`polish_full`:
```
def polish_full(h0, deadline):
    n=len(h0); best=project_feasible(h0.copy(),n); best_c=eval_c5(best); x=best.copy()
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    alphas=(5.0,20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0)
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > deadline-20: break
       try: res=minimize(full_fun, x, args=(alpha,100.0), bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':150,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-10})
       except: continue
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): continue
       x2=project_feasible(res.x,n); c2=eval_c5(x2)
       if c2<best_c: best=x2; best_c=c2; x=x2.copy()
    return best,best_c
```

Let's draft compact code now in analysis to count lines.

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)
    for _ in range(100):
        d = target - x.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13: break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - x; s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-14: break
            x = x + d * r / s
        else:
            s = x.sum()
            if s < 1e-14: break
            x = x + d * x / s
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

def project_feasible(h, n):
    return project_sum(h, 0.5 * n)

def eval_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * 2.0 / n)

def build_sym_h(a, half):
    s = np.concatenate((a, a[::-1]))
    return np.concatenate((s, s))

def cross_vals(h, n):
    vals = np.empty(2*n - 1)
    om = 1.0 - h
    vals[0] = np.dot(h, om)
    idx = 1
    for L in range(1, n):
        m = n - L
        vals[idx] = np.dot(h[:m], om[L:]); idx += 1
        vals[idx] = np.dot(h[L:], om[:m]); idx += 1
    return vals

def cross_grad(p, h, n):
    om = 1.0 - h
    g = np.zeros(n)
    p0 = p[0]
    if p0:
        g += p0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    idx = 1
    for L in range(1, n):
        m = n - L
        pp = p[idx]; pn = p[idx + 1]; idx += 2
        if pp:
            g[:m] += pp * om[L:]
            g[L:] += -pp * h[:m]
        if pn:
            g[L:] += pn * om[:m]
            g[:m] += -pn * h[L:]
    return g

def softmax_counts(vals, alpha):
    m = float(vals.max())
    s = alpha * (vals - m)
    sm = float(s.max())
    e = np.exp(s - sm)
    t = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(t) or t <= 0:
        p = np.zeros_like(vals)
        act = np.nonzero(vals == m)[0]
        p[act] = 1.0 / len(act)
        return m, p
    p = e / t
    return m + (sm + np.log(t)) / alpha, p

def sym_fun(a, alpha, penalty, half):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    half = int(half)
    h = build_sym_h(x, half)
    n = 4 * half
    dx = 1.0 / (2.0 * half)
    vals = cross_vals(h, n)
    oc, p = softmax_counts(vals, alpha)
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * half
    obj = dx * oc + penalty * diff * diff
    gc = cross_grad(p, h, n)
    gs = gc[:2*half] + gc[2*half:]
    ga = gs[:half] + gs[::-1][:half]
    return obj, dx * ga + 2.0 * penalty * diff

def full_fun(h, alpha, penalty):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = len(x)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    vals = cross_vals(x, n)
    oc, p = softmax_counts(vals, alpha)
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
    obj = dx * oc + penalty * diff * diff
    return obj, dx * cross_grad(p, x, n) + 2.0 * penalty * diff

def polish_sym_seed(a, half, deadline):
    x = project_sum(a, 0.5 * half)
    best_a = x.copy()
    best_h = build_sym_h(x, half)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * half
    for alpha in (5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
        try:
            res = minimize(sym_fun, x, args=(alpha, 100.0, half), bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': 150, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-10})
            x2 = res.x
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x2)): continue
        x2 = project_sum(x2, 0.5 * half)
        h2 = build_sym_h(x2, half)
        c2 = eval_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_a = x2.copy(); best_h = h2; best_c = c2; x = x2.copy()
    return best_a, best_h, best_c

def polish_full(h0, deadline):
    n = len(h0)
    best = project_feasible(h0.copy(), n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best)
    x = best.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for alpha in (5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
        try:
            res = minimize(full_fun, x, args=(alpha, 100.0), bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': 120, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-10})
            x2 = res.x
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x2)): continue
        x2 = project_feasible(x2, n)
        c2 = eval_c5(x2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best = x2; best_c = c2; x = x2.copy()
    return best, best_c

def fix_count(b, target, rng):
    b = b.astype(float).copy()
    cnt = int(b.sum())
    if cnt > target:
        idx = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
        if idx.size:
            sel = rng.choice(idx, cnt - target, replace=False)
            b[sel] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        idx = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
        if idx.size:
            sel = rng.choice(idx, target - cnt, replace=False)
            b[sel] = 1.0
    return b

def random_bits(nbits, target, rng):
    b = np.zeros(nbits)
    b[rng.choice(nbits, target, replace=False)] = 1.0
    return b

def markov_bits(nbits, p, target, rng):
    b = np.empty(nbits)
    b[0] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else 0.0
    for i in range(1, nbits):
        b[i] = (1.0 - b[i-1]) if rng.rand() < p else b[i-1]
    return fix_count(b, target, rng)

def add_candidate(cc, ca, a, half):
    a = project_sum(a, 0.5 * half)
    h = build_sym_h(a, half)
    c = eval_c5(h)
    if np.isfinite(c):
        cc.append(c); ca.append(a)
    return c

def a_to_bits(a, half):
    target = half // 2
    idx = np.argsort(-np.asarray(a, dtype=float))
    b = np.zeros(half)
    b[idx[:target]] = 1.0
    return b

def set_sym_bit(h, idx, val, half):
    H = 2 * half
    n = 4 * half
    h[idx] = val
    h[H - 1 - idx] = val
    h[H + idx] = val
    h[n - 1 - idx] = val

def binary_search(a0, half, deadline, rng):
    a = a0.astype(float).copy()
    h = build_sym_h(a, half)
    dx = 1.0 / (2.0 * half)
    cur = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
    best_c = cur
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    T = 0.01
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        ones = np.nonzero(a > 0.5)[0]
        zeros = np.nonzero(a < 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        i = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
        j = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
        if i == j: continue
        oi = float(a[i]); oj = float(a[j])
        ni = 1.0 - oi; nj = 1.0 - oj
        set_sym_bit(h, i, ni, half)
        set_sym_bit(h, j, nj, half)
        c2 = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
        ok = c2 < cur
        if not ok:
            d = c2 - cur
            if d <= T and d >= 0.0 and rng.rand() < np.exp(-d / T):
                ok = True
        if ok:
            a[i] = ni; a[j] = nj
            cur = c2
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2; best_a = a.copy(); best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            set_sym_bit(h, i, oi, half)
            set_sym_bit(h, j, oj, half)
            stall += 1
        if stall > 250:
            if rng.rand() < 0.6 or best_c <= cur:
                a = best_a.copy(); h = best_h.copy(); cur = best_c
            else:
                a = best_a.copy(); h = best_h.copy(); cur = best_c
                ones = np.nonzero(a > 0.5)[0]; zeros = np.nonzero(a < 0.5)[0]
                if ones.size and zeros.size:
                    p1 = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)]); p2 = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
                    if p1 != p2:
                        oi = float(a[p1]); oj = float(a[p2])
                        set_sym_bit(h, p1, 1.0 - oi, half); set_sym_bit(h, p2, 1.0 - oj, half)
                        a[p1] = 1.0 - oi; a[p2] = 1.0 - oj
                        cur = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
            stall = 0
            T = 0.01
        else:
            T = max(0.0005, T * 0.9995)
    return best_h, best_c, best_a

def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    half = 100
    n = 4 * half
    h_par = None
    try:
        hp = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if hp.size != n:
            x_old = np.arange(hp.size) * (2.0 / hp.size)
            x_new = np.arange(n) * (2.0 / n)
            hp = np.interp(x_new, x_old, hp)
        h_par = project_feasible(hp, n)
    except Exception:
        h_par = None
    if h_par is None:
        a0 = random_bits(half, half // 2, rng)
        best_h = build_sym_h(a0, half)
        best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
        a_par = a0
    else:
        best_h = h_par.copy()
        best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
        a_par = (best_h[:half] + best_h[half:2*half][::-1] + best_h[2*half:3*half] + best_h[3*half:4*half][::-1]) * 0.25
    best_a = project_sum(a_par, 0.5 * half)
    if budget_s < 60:
        return best_h, float(best_c), n
    cc = []; ca = []
    add_candidate(cc, ca, best_a, half)
    gen_deadline = min(t0 + 120.0, deadline - 550.0)
    for _ in range(250):
        if time.time() >= gen_deadline or len(cc) > 4000: break
        add_candidate(cc, ca, random_bits(half, half // 2, rng), half)
    ps = (0.015, 0.02, 0.025, 0.03, 0.04, 0.05, 0.06, 0.08)
    for p in ps:
        for _ in range(400):
            if time.time() >= gen_deadline or len(cc) > 4000: break
            add_candidate(cc, ca, markov_bits(half, p, half // 2, rng), half)
    if cc:
        arr_c = np.array(cc)
        order = np.argsort(arr_c)
        if arr_c[order[0]] < best_c:
            # update maybe top? Not necessary, seeds include
            pass
        top = [ca[i] for i in order[:8]]
    else:
        top = [best_a]
    # sym polish
    for a_seed in top:
        if time.time() > deadline - 480: break
        rem = min(250.0, deadline - 480 - time.time())
        if rem <= 15: break
        a2, h2, c2 = polish_sym_seed(a_seed, half, time.time() + rem)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2; best_h = h2; best_a = a2
    # full polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 320:
        fd = min(deadline - 320, time.time() + 250.0)
        h2, c2 = polish_full(best_h, fd)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2; best_h = h2
    # binary
    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        bits_list = []
        if best_a is not None:
            bits_list.append(a_to_bits(best_a, half))
        if best_h.size == n:
            ah = (best_h[:half] + best_h[half:2*half][::-1] + best_h[2*half:3*half] + best_h[3*half:4*half][::-1]) * 0.25
            ah = project_sum(ah, 0.5 * half)
            bits_list.append(a_to_bits(ah, half))
        b0 = bits_list[0]
        if len(bits_list) > 1:
            c1 = eval_c5(build_sym_h(bits_list[0], half))
            c2b = eval_c5(build_sym_h(bits_list[1], half))
            if c2b < c1:
                b0 = bits_list[1]
        h2, c2, a2 = binary_search(b0, half, deadline - 25, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2; best_h = h2; best_a = a2
    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), n
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def project_sum(x, target):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(x, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)
    for _ in range(100):
        d = target - x.sum()
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        if d > 0:
            r = 1.0 - x
            s = r.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            x = x + d * r / s
        else:
            s = x.sum()
            if s < 1e-14:
                break
            x = x + d * x / s
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    return project_sum(h, 0.5 * n)


def eval_c5(h):
    n = len(h)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * 2.0 / n)


def build_sym_h(a, half):
    s = np.concatenate((a, a[::-1]))
    return np.concatenate((s, s))


def cross_vals(h, n):
    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1)
    om = 1.0 - h
    vals[0] = np.dot(h, om)
    idx = 1
    for L in range(1, n):
        m = n - L
        vals[idx] = np.dot(h[:m], om[L:])
        idx += 1
        vals[idx] = np.dot(h[L:], om[:m])
        idx += 1
    return vals


def cross_grad(p, h, n):
    om = 1.0 - h
    g = np.zeros(n)
    p0 = p[0]
    if p0:
        g += p0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    idx = 1
    for L in range(1, n):
        m = n - L
        pp = p[idx]
        pn = p[idx + 1]
        idx += 2
        if pp:
            g[:m] += pp * om[L:]
            g[L:] += -pp * h[:m]
        if pn:
            g[L:] += pn * om[:m]
            g[:m] += -pn * h[L:]
    return g


def softmax_counts(vals, alpha):
    m = float(vals.max())
    s = alpha * (vals - m)
    sm = float(s.max())
    e = np.exp(s - sm)
    t = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(t) or t <= 0:
        p = np.zeros_like(vals)
        act = np.nonzero(vals == m)[0]
        p[act] = 1.0 / len(act)
        return m, p
    p = e / t
    return m + (sm + np.log(t)) / alpha, p


def sym_fun(a, alpha, penalty, half):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    half = int(half)
    h = build_sym_h(x, half)
    n = 4 * half
    dx = 1.0 / (2.0 * half)
    vals = cross_vals(h, n)
    oc, p = softmax_counts(vals, alpha)
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * half
    obj = dx * oc + penalty * diff * diff
    gc = cross_grad(p, h, n)
    gs = gc[:2 * half] + gc[2 * half:]
    ga = gs[:half] + gs[::-1][:half]
    return obj, dx * ga + 2.0 * penalty * diff


def full_fun(h, alpha, penalty):
    x = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = len(x)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    vals = cross_vals(x, n)
    oc, p = softmax_counts(vals, alpha)
    diff = x.sum() - 0.5 * n
    obj = dx * oc + penalty * diff * diff
    return obj, dx * cross_grad(p, x, n) + 2.0 * penalty * diff


def polish_sym_seed(a, half, deadline):
    x = project_sum(a, 0.5 * half)
    best_a = x.copy()
    best_h = build_sym_h(x, half)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * half
    for alpha in (5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 10:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(sym_fun, x, args=(alpha, 100.0, half),
                           bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B',
                           options={'maxiter': 150, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-10})
            x2 = res.x
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x2)):
            continue
        x2 = project_sum(x2, 0.5 * half)
        h2 = build_sym_h(x2, half)
        c2 = eval_c5(h2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_a = x2.copy()
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2
            x = x2.copy()
    return best_a, best_h, best_c


def polish_full(h0, deadline):
    n = len(h0)
    best = project_feasible(h0.copy(), n)
    best_c = eval_c5(best)
    x = best.copy()
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    for alpha in (5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 20:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(full_fun, x, args=(alpha, 100.0),
                           bounds=bounds, method='L-BFGS-B',
                           options={'maxiter': 120, 'ftol': 1e-14, 'gtol': 1e-10})
            x2 = res.x
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x2)):
            continue
        x2 = project_feasible(x2, n)
        c2 = eval_c5(x2)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best = x2
            best_c = c2
            x = x2.copy()
    return best, best_c


def fix_count(b, target, rng):
    b = b.astype(float).copy()
    cnt = int(b.sum())
    if cnt > target:
        idx = np.nonzero(b > 0.5)[0]
        if idx.size:
            sel = rng.choice(idx, cnt - target, replace=False)
            b[sel] = 0.0
    elif cnt < target:
        idx = np.nonzero(b < 0.5)[0]
        if idx.size:
            sel = rng.choice(idx, target - cnt, replace=False)
            b[sel] = 1.0
    return b


def random_bits(nbits, target, rng):
    b = np.zeros(nbits)
    b[rng.choice(nbits, target, replace=False)] = 1.0
    return b


def markov_bits(nbits, p, target, rng):
    b = np.empty(nbits)
    b[0] = 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else 0.0
    for i in range(1, nbits):
        b[i] = (1.0 - b[i - 1]) if rng.rand() < p else b[i - 1]
    return fix_count(b, target, rng)


def add_candidate(cc, ca, a, half):
    a = project_sum(a, 0.5 * half)
    h = build_sym_h(a, half)
    c = eval_c5(h)
    if np.isfinite(c):
        cc.append(c)
        ca.append(a)
    return c


def a_to_bits(a, half):
    target = half // 2
    idx = np.argsort(-np.asarray(a, dtype=float))
    b = np.zeros(half)
    b[idx[:target]] = 1.0
    return b


def set_sym_bit(h, idx, val, half):
    H = 2 * half
    n = 4 * half
    h[idx] = val
    h[H - 1 - idx] = val
    h[H + idx] = val
    h[n - 1 - idx] = val


def binary_search(a0, half, deadline, rng):
    a = a0.astype(float).copy()
    h = build_sym_h(a, half)
    dx = 1.0 / (2.0 * half)
    cur = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
    best_c = cur
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    T = 0.01
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        ones = np.nonzero(a > 0.5)[0]
        zeros = np.nonzero(a < 0.5)[0]
        if ones.size == 0 or zeros.size == 0:
            break
        i = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
        j = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
        if i == j:
            continue
        oi = float(a[i])
        oj = float(a[j])
        ni = 1.0 - oi
        nj = 1.0 - oj
        set_sym_bit(h, i, ni, half)
        set_sym_bit(h, j, nj, half)
        c2 = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
        ok = c2 < cur
        if not ok:
            d = c2 - cur
            if d >= 0.0 and d <= T and rng.rand() < np.exp(-d / T):
                ok = True
        if ok:
            a[i] = ni
            a[j] = nj
            cur = c2
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_h = h.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            set_sym_bit(h, i, oi, half)
            set_sym_bit(h, j, oj, half)
            stall += 1
        if stall > 250:
            a = best_a.copy()
            h = best_h.copy()
            cur = best_c
            if rng.rand() > 0.5:
                ones = np.nonzero(a > 0.5)[0]
                zeros = np.nonzero(a < 0.5)[0]
                if ones.size and zeros.size:
                    p1 = int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
                    p2 = int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)])
                    if p1 != p2:
                        oi = float(a[p1])
                        oj = float(a[p2])
                        set_sym_bit(h, p1, 1.0 - oi, half)
                        set_sym_bit(h, p2, 1.0 - oj, half)
                        a[p1] = 1.0 - oi
                        a[p2] = 1.0 - oj
                        cur = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * dx)
            stall = 0
            T = 0.01
        else:
            T = max(0.0005, T * 0.9995)
    return best_h, best_c, best_a


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(10.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    half = 100
    n = 4 * half

    h_par = None
    try:
        hp = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if hp.size != n:
            x_old = np.arange(hp.size) * (2.0 / hp.size)
            x_new = np.arange(n) * (2.0 / n)
            hp = np.interp(x_new, x_old, hp)
        h_par = project_feasible(hp, n)
    except Exception:
        h_par = None

    if h_par is None:
        a0 = random_bits(half, half // 2, rng)
        best_h = build_sym_h(a0, half)
        best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
        a_par = a0
    else:
        best_h = h_par.copy()
        best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
        a_par = (best_h[:half] + best_h[half:2 * half][::-1]
                 + best_h[2 * half:3 * half] + best_h[3 * half:4 * half][::-1]) * 0.25
    best_a = project_sum(a_par, 0.5 * half)

    if budget_s < 60:
        return best_h, float(best_c), n

    cc = []
    ca = []
    add_candidate(cc, ca, best_a, half)

    gen_deadline = min(t0 + 120.0, deadline - 550.0)
    for _ in range(250):
        if time.time() >= gen_deadline or len(cc) > 4000:
            break
        add_candidate(cc, ca, random_bits(half, half // 2, rng), half)

    ps = (0.01, 0.015, 0.02, 0.025, 0.03, 0.04, 0.05, 0.07)
    for p in ps:
        for _ in range(400):
            if time.time() >= gen_deadline or len(cc) > 4000:
                break
            add_candidate(cc, ca, markov_bits(half, p, half // 2, rng), half)

    top = [best_a]
    if cc:
        arr_c = np.array(cc)
        order = np.argsort(arr_c)
        for i in order[:20]:
            if arr_c[i] < best_c:
                best_c = float(arr_c[i])
                best_a = ca[i]
                best_h = build_sym_h(best_a, half)
        top = []
        for i in order[:8]:
            top.append(ca[i])

    for a_seed in top:
        if time.time() > deadline - 480:
            break
        rem = min(250.0, deadline - 480 - time.time())
        if rem <= 15:
            break
        a2, h2, c2 = polish_sym_seed(a_seed, half, time.time() + rem)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
            best_a = a2

    if time.time() < deadline - 320:
        fd = min(deadline - 320, time.time() + 250.0)
        h2, c2 = polish_full(best_h, fd)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2

    if time.time() < deadline - 25:
        bits_list = []
        if best_a is not None:
            bits_list.append(a_to_bits(best_a, half))
        if best_h.size == n:
            ah = (best_h[:half] + best_h[half:2 * half][::-1]
                  + best_h[2 * half:3 * half] + best_h[3 * half:4 * half][::-1]) * 0.25
            ah = project_sum(ah, 0.5 * half)
            bits_list.append(a_to_bits(ah, half))
        b0 = bits_list[0]
        if len(bits_list) > 1:
            c1 = eval_c5(build_sym_h(bits_list[0], half))
            c2b = eval_c5(build_sym_h(bits_list[1], half))
            if c2b < c1:
                b0 = bits_list[1]
        h2, c2, a2 = binary_search(b0, half, deadline - 25, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2
            best_a = a2

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    best_c = eval_c5(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), n
```
